9b2(tog gzungs 2): zla nya 'dra //skar ma rnams kyis yongs bskor bzhin //thub sku gser gyi kha dog la //mtshan rnams kyis ni yongs su bskor //'jig rten mes po tshangs pa yis /'jig rten sgron mas
